  28. تركيب الاستعلام
  بدل PRODUCT + supplier فقط، استخدم:
  1 Product Term + 1 Role Term + 1 Commercial Term + 1 Access/Proof Term
  مثال:
  PRODUCT + distributor + price list
  PRODUCT + reseller + private catalog
  PRODUCT + supplier + API
  PRODUCT + wholesaler + partner pricing
  PRODUCT + H2H + pricing
  PRODUCT + dealer price + catalog
  PRODUCT + bulk price + MOQ
  29. مصفوفة الاستعلام
  لكل SKU:
  PRODUCT × {supplier, wholesaler, distributor, reseller, dealer, master distributor}
  ثم:
  PRODUCT × {wholesale price, reseller price, dealer price, net price, bulk price, tier price, partner price}
  ثم:
  PRODUCT × {private catalog, closed catalog, reseller portal, partner portal, API, H2H}
  ثم:
  SELLER × {supplier, source, distributor, upstream, fulfilled by, powered by}
  ثم:
  DOMAIN × {related domains, API, partner, reseller, distributor}
  ثم:
  USERNAME/CHANNEL × {supplier, source, wholesale, reseller, distributor, bot}
  30. نظام البذور
  كل نتيجة جديدة تتحول إلى Seed:
  Product Seed، Seller Seed، Supplier Seed، Domain Seed، Username Seed، Channel Seed، Bot Seed، SKU Seed، Price Seed، API Seed، Catalog Seed، Document Seed .
  التسلسل:
  Seed → Query Expansion → New Entity → Entity Resolution → Relation Search → Upstream Search → Price Search → Verification
  31. منع التكرار
  قبل تسجيل جهة جديدة، افحص:
  domain، company، URL، username، Telegram ID، support contact، product catalog، SKU، region، currency، price pattern، API pattern، description fingerprint .
  لا تدمج جهتين لمجرد تشابه الاسم أو التصميم.
  32. قاموس التحقق
  official، authorized، verified، approved، certified، registered، partner، distributor، direct، indirect، primary، upstream، source، documented، published، quoted، account-only، private، negotiated، observed، checkout، transaction، invoice، proof، evidence، terms، policy، agreement، contract.
  33. قاموس الأدلة
  invoice، purchase invoice، receipt، order confirmation، quote، quotation، contract، reseller agreement، distribution agreement، authorization letter، partner directory، official partner listing، product catalog، price list، account evidence، checkout evidence، API documentation، terms of service، reseller terms، support response، business registration، company profile.
  34. البحث المضاد بعد العثور على مورد
  نفّذ:
  PRODUCT + supplier name
  PRODUCT + supplier domain
  supplier + distributor
  supplier + wholesaler
  supplier + upstream
  supplier + partner
  supplier + reseller
  supplier + API
  supplier + catalog
  supplier + price list
  supplier + PDF
  supplier + Telegram
  supplier + username
  supplier + support
  35. البحث عن مورد بديل أو مصدر مشترك
  عند العثور على Supplier A:
  "PRODUCT" "Supplier A"
  "PRODUCT" "same price"
  "PRODUCT" "same SKU"
  "PRODUCT" "same catalog"
  "PRODUCT" "same region"
  "PRODUCT" "Supplier A" reseller
  "PRODUCT" "Supplier A" distributor
  "Supplier A" partner
  "Supplier A" API
  "Supplier A" Telegram
  "Supplier A" wholesale
  الهدف:
  parallel supplier، alternative distributor، common upstream، shared inventory، duplicate catalog .
  36. قاعدة البحث المكثف
  لا تستخدم آلاف الكلمات في استعلام واحد. أنشئ مجموعات صغيرة مستقلة، ثم اجمع النتائج.
  أفضل تركيب عملي:
  Product + Role + Commercial + Access/Proof
  37. مخرجات المساعد البحثي
  لكل نتيجة:
  Entity → Role → Product/SKU → Region → Price → Price Type → Availability → Guarantee → Resale Status → Upstream Claim → Upstream Evidence → Source Confidence → Price Confidence → URL → Last Checked → Next Search Seed
  38. قاعدة التوقف
  لا تتوقف عند العثور على مورد.
  تتوقف دورة البحث عندما  تُستنفد مسارات الاكتشاف المعقولة، وتُحل التكرارات، وتُفحص العلاقات، وتُوثق الحدود، وتُسجل أسباب توقف التتبع .
  39. حدود الاستخدام
  المعجم مخصص لاكتشاف المصادر والموردين والأسعار العامة أو التجارية القابلة للوصول المشروع. لا يشمل البحث عن كلمات مرور أو مفاتيح API مسربة أو تجاوز أنظمة الدخول أو استخراج بيانات خاصة دون تصريح.
  40. الخلاصة
  المعجم يحوّل البحث من  Supplier Search  إلى:
  Role Discovery → Commercial Discovery → Access Discovery → Source Discovery → Relationship Discovery → Recursive Expansion → Price Discovery → Evidence Discovery → Multilingual Expansion → Verification .
  القاعدة الذهبية:
  كل نتيجة = معلومة + علاقة + بذرة بحث جديدة.
  ولا يوجد مصطلح واحد يكشف المورد الحقيقي. أقوى النتائج تأتي من  تقاطع عدة إشارات مستقلة : الدور، السعر، الكتالوج، API، H2H، الحساب، القناة، النطاق، النص، المنطقة، والوثيقة.
المواصفة التشغيلية — البحث العميق العالمي عن الموردين ومصادرهم
  الهدف
  هذا المستند يحول البحث من العثور على متاجر إلى نظام استكشاف سوق وسلسلة توريد رقمية.
  Discovery Breadth + Discovery Depth + Source Coverage + Price Coverage + Evidence Coverage
  1. فصل السياق عن البحث
  بنية المشروع مرجع لفهم السياق والمنهج فقط. لا تُستخدم كمادة بحثية، ولا تُعتبر بياناتها دليلًا خارجيًا. أي بيانات سابقة تستخدم فقط كبذور، ثم يعاد التحقق منها خارجيًا.
  2. التوسع التكراري
  كل رابط أو متجر أو Channel أو Group أو Account أو Bot أو API أو Domain هو  Seed  جديد.
  Seed → Search Expansion → Entity Discovery → Entity Resolution → Relation Discovery → Upstream Search → Price Search → Evidence Search → Cross-Validation → New Seeds → Repeat
  3. خريطة سلسلة التوريد
  Seller → Reseller → Wholesaler → Master/Deep Wholesale → Distributor → Aggregator → Upstream Supplier → Primary/Original Source
  لا تفترض وجود طبقة غير مثبتة، ولا تجعل المصدر الأعلى مساويًا تلقائيًا للأرخص.
  4. مسارات الاكتشاف
  Product-first، Seller-first، Price-first، Channel-first، Bot-first، Username-first، Domain-first، API-first، SKU-first، Marketplace-first، Catalog-first، Document-first، Historical-first، Language-first، Infrastructure-first، Community-first، Advertisement-first، Referral-first.
  5. هندسة الاستعلام
  استخدم تركيبًا متعدد الطبقات:
  Product + Role + Commercial Term + Access/Proof Term
  أمثلة:
  PRODUCT + distributor + price list
  PRODUCT + reseller + private catalog
  PRODUCT + wholesaler + partner pricing
  PRODUCT + supplier + API
  PRODUCT + H2H + pricing
  PRODUCT + dealer + net price
  PRODUCT + bulk + MOQ
  PRODUCT + distributor + custom quote
  6. معجم الأدوار
  supplier، vendor، wholesaler، distributor، sub-distributor، master distributor، deep wholesaler، reseller، dealer، aggregator، indirect provider، indirect reseller، upstream supplier، primary source.
  7. معجم الأسعار
  wholesale price، reseller price، dealer price، partner price، distributor price، net price، buying cost، acquisition cost، B2B price، bulk price، volume price، tier price، private price، negotiated price، custom quote، RFQ.
  8. معجم الوصول المغلق
  private catalog، closed catalog، private wholesale، private pricing، partner-only pricing، reseller portal، dealer portal، distributor portal، supplier portal، B2B portal، login required، approval required، request catalog، request pricing، contact wholesale.
  9. معجم المصدر الأعلى
  supplier behind supplier، supplier of supplier، supplier's supplier، upstream supplier، upstream distributor، upstream wholesaler، next upstream supplier، higher-tier distributor، master distributor behind، wholesaler behind، backend supplier، inventory provider، fulfillment provider، who powers this reseller، who supplies this seller، where this inventory comes from.
  10. API / H2H
  reseller API، wholesale API، B2B API، partner API، distributor API، catalog API، pricing API، inventory API، fulfillment API، order API، supplier API، aggregator API، white-label API، webhook، H2H، Host-to-Host، H2H pricing، H2H wholesale، H2H supplier، H2H integration.
  11. Telegram
  Channel / Group / Bot / Account / Post / Username / Support Contact  = عقد اكتشاف.
  قوالب:
  site: t.me  PRODUCT wholesale
  site: t.me  PRODUCT reseller
  site: t.me  PRODUCT supplier
  site: t.me  PRODUCT distributor
  site: t.me  PRODUCT H2H
  site: t.me  PRODUCT API
  site: t.me  PRODUCT price
  site: t.me  PRODUCT source
  بعد اكتشاف قناة:  CHANNEL + supplier / distributor / source / reseller / wholesale / API / bot / support / partner .
  12. البحث العكسي من السعر أو المحتوى
  عند العثور على سعر منخفض أو وصف متكرر، ابحث عن:
  PRICE + PRODUCT ،  PRICE + SKU ،  PRICE + REGION ،  Exact Product Title ،  Exact Product Description ،  Exact Price Phrase ،  Exact FAQ ،  Exact SKU .
  الهدف اكتشاف جهات موازية أو مصدر مشترك، لا إثبات العلاقة بمجرد التشابه.
  13. Domain / Username Discovery
  related domains، associated domains، same certificate، same infrastructure، same IP، same ASN، same nameserver، same DNS، same analytics ID، same tracking ID، same payment provider، same support email، same Telegram، same username، same catalog.
  Username: sales username، support username، reseller username، wholesale contact، partner contact، admin، owner، manager، account manager.
  هذه إشارات اكتشاف فقط، وليست إثباتًا للعلاقة التجارية.
  14. التحقق
  كل ادعاء:
  Claim → Evidence → Verification Status
  افصل بين:
  Displayed Price / Account Price / VIP-Dealer-Distributor Price / Volume Price / Negotiated Price / Checkout Price / Effective Acquisition Cost / Actual Transaction Price.
  15. المطابقة السعرية
  لا تقارن إلا بعد تثبيت:
  Brand، Product، Plan/Edition، Duration، Quantity/Face Value، Region، Activation، Account Type، Delivery، Restrictions .
  16. الضمان وإعادة البيع
  الضمان:
  Documented Guarantee / Explicitly No Guarantee / No Documented Guarantee / Guarantee Unknown .
  إعادة البيع:
  Authorized / Resale Terms Available / Unclear / Restricted / Unknown .
  السعر ≠ الضمان ≠ المصدر ≠ حق إعادة البيع.
  17. المخرج لكل نتيجة
  Entity → Role → Supply Level → Product/SKU → Region → Price → Price Type → Effective Cost → Availability → Guarantee → Resale Status → Upstream Claim → Upstream Evidence → Price Verification → Source Verification → Evidence URLs → Last Checked → Next Search Seeds
  18. المخرج لكل SKU
  Cheapest Advertised، Cheapest Direct/Observed، Cheapest Without Documented Guarantee، Cheapest With Documented Guarantee، Known Upstream Supplier، Primary/Original Source عند ثبوته، Effective Acquisition Cost، Availability، Evidence، Last Checked، Exclusions.
  19. البحث المستقل بين ChatGPT  وz.ai
  نفس الـSeeds ونفس تعريف الـSKU ونفس معايير التحقق، مع منع كشف نتائج النظام الأول للثاني قبل انتهاء الجولة المستقلة.
  المقارنة تكون على:
  Unique Suppliers، Unique Upstream Sources، Unique Prices، Verified Relationships، Discovery Depth، New Discovery Paths، Duplicate Rate، Evidence Coverage .
  20. معيار «الأرخص»
  لا تستخدم «الأرخص في العالم» كحقيقة.
  الصياغة المعتمدة:
  «أقل سعر تم اكتشافه والتحقق منه ضمن نطاق البحث وتاريخ الفحص والشروط المحددة للـSKU» .
  21. قاعدة التوقف
  لا توقف البحث عند العثور على مورد. أوقف  المسار المحدد  عندما تتوقف عمليات البحث المستقلة عن إنتاج جهات أو علاقات أو أسعار أو أدلة جديدة، مع تسجيل سبب التوقف.
  22. الاختبار النقدي
  راقب دائمًا: عدم إمكانية إثبات المصدر الأعلى، الأسعار الخاصة غير القابلة للتعميم، اختلاف الـSKU، التكرار، الاستنتاج من التشابه التقني، قيود إعادة البيع، وتقادم الأسعار والمخزون.
  23. الحدود
  العمل على المعلومات القابلة للوصول والتحقق المشروع. لا تتضمن المهمة كلمات مرور أو مفاتيح API مسربة أو تجاوز أنظمة الدخول أو استخراج بيانات خاصة دون تصريح.
  24. الصيغة النهائية
  Canonical SKU → Multi-Path Discovery → Recursive Expansion → Entity Resolution → Supplier Graph → SKU Matching → Price Extraction → Cost Normalization → Guarantee Verification → Resale Verification → Upstream Tracing → Cross-Validation → Evidence Capture → Final Classification → Last Checked → Re-search
  25. معيار النجاح
  النجاح =  أكبر عدد من الجهات الفريدة + أكبر عمق مثبت في سلسلة التوريد + أكبر تغطية سعرية قابلة للمقارنة + أكبر تغطية للأدلة + أكبر عدد من الاكتشافات الجديدة بين المسارات المستقلة .
  هذه المواصفة هي المرجع التشغيلي المتخصص لهذا الموضوع، ولا تحول أي Lead أو ادعاء أو سعر غير متحقق إلى حقيقة معتمدة.

  المبدأ الحاكم

  لا تُعتمد أي إضافة لمجرد أنها أكثر تعقيدًا أو أطول.
  الهدف النهائي هو بناء:
  Adaptive Supplier Intelligence Research Engine

  ChatGPT Workspace — Review & Analysis
  Role
  Review · Audit · Critical Analysis · Testing · Validation
  This workspace is owned by the ChatGPT review function.
  Purpose
  - Review Claude proposals and supplier-research architecture.
  - Run Paper Tests and Stress Tests.
  - Detect defects, contradictions, missing requirements, and unsupported assumptions.
  - Maintain review findings without silently changing Canonical content.
  Authority
  Review ≠ Authority.
  This workspace is owned by the ChatGPT review function.
  ChatGPT may edit this workspace's analysis, audits, tests, findings, repair records, operational state, and project-linked working records.
  ChatGPT may not:
  - edit the Claude Workspace;
  - directly rewrite Claude's Prompt Working Reference;
  - mark a Prompt Approved or promote a Candidate;
  - silently alter governance or ownership rules;
  - delete historical records;
  - silently merge competing Candidates;
  - replace authoritative Prompt text with an inferred reconstruction.
  Prompt Text changes belong to the  Prompt Working Reference , which is Claude-owned for direct editing. ChatGPT reviews those changes and records findings here.
  Standard review output
  Finding → Evidence → Impact → Recommendation → Status
  Allowed finding states:
  - Open
  - Accepted
  - Repaired
  - Rejected
  - Needs User Decision
  Handoff to Claude
  Send only the material required for the next Claude revision:
  - Current Canonical Version ID
  - Review findings
  - Required repairs
  - Open decisions
  - Tests to rerun
  Do not assume Claude has access to this workspace unless the relevant content is explicitly transferred.
  Notion-First Relay Protocol
  Notion is the durable record; chat is transport only.
  For each cycle:
  - Archive the User Input.
  - Archive ChatGPT's analysis and current state.
  - Prepare exactly one copy-ready Claude Handoff.
  - After Claude responds, archive the complete response before analysis.
  - Audit the response against the current Candidate/Canonical state.
  - Record findings, repairs, open decisions, and next action.
  - Prepare the next single Claude Handoff.
  Claude must not be given the full historical transcript unless a specific test requires it. The Handoff Packet is the controlled context transfer.
  🧪 Runtime Evidence —  z.ai  v4.1 Candidate / Multi-SKU Catalog Test — 2026-09-27
  Source
  User-provided screenshots from  z.ai  GLM-5.3 runtime after loading  ADAPTIVE SUPPLIER INTELLIGENCE RESEARCH ENGINE — v4.1 CANDIDATE .
  Confirmed Runtime Observations
  - The runtime transitioned from input-gated state to an executing Run Contract after the user supplied the required scope information.
  - The runtime represented the scope as  Run +400 SKUs , with a catalog of more than 400 digital products/services.
  - The runtime grouped execution into batches and explicitly treated  Batch 1  as a partial coverage unit rather than claiming that 400 SKUs were fully researched in one batch.
  - The runtime applied  C4 / 96 Research Actions  to the active batch and referenced a  25% reserve  and  35% per-branch backstop .
  - The runtime exposed a multi-scenario evaluation model: 1 Unit, 2–10 Units, Bulk, plus commercial tiers such as Volume/VIP/Dealer/Distributor/Partner/MOQ/Negotiated where applicable.
  - The runtime retained global supplier discovery with region-aware SKU matching.
  - The runtime preserved  Known-Facts vs Open-Items  and treated historical seeds as  Unverified Leads  requiring re-checking.
  - Batch 1 began with multiple product/service families rather than a single product.
  Critical Validation Finding
  The current Runtime evidence validates the existence of a  catalog-scale / batch execution concept , but it does  not  validate complete research coverage of 400+ SKUs within a single 96-action C4 batch. The runtime itself indicates that Batch 1 is only a subset and that complete 400-SKU verification cannot be claimed from one batch.
  Architecture Implication
  The correct scalable model is:
  Catalog Program (>400 SKUs) → Batches → Per-SKU research units → Shared global coverage registry → Cross-batch deduplication/entity resolution → Aggregate catalog intelligence.
  A batch budget must be interpreted as a budget for the active batch, not as proof that the entire >400-SKU catalog has been deeply researched.
  Proposed Technical Repair — NOT a Governance Decision
  The next Prompt Candidate should explicitly model:
  - Catalog-level scope  separately from  Batch-level Run Contract .
  - Per-SKU completion state  independently from Batch completion.
  - Coverage Ledger  across all 400+ SKUs.
  - Batch budget allocation  with cumulative program accounting.
  - Unsearched / Partially Researched / Verified / Retrieval-Limited / Budget-Limited  state per SKU.
  - No final catalog-wide claim of completeness unless every scoped SKU has an explicit terminal state and the aggregate coverage record supports the claim.
  - Cross-batch deduplication and supplier/entity relationships must persist across the catalog program.
  Status
  Review Finding — Open / Requires Claude Prompt Revision Review
  This record is runtime evidence and a proposed technical repair. It does not modify the Claude-owned Prompt Working Reference or Canonical approval state.
  🔎 Cycle 5 — ChatGPT Audit of Claude Response — 2026-09-27
  Assessment
  Claude's response is structurally consistent with the current project direction and correctly identifies the central v4.1 catalog-scale gap: v4.1 models a single research run/SKU lifecycle but does not explicitly model a multi-SKU catalog program with batches and cross-batch state.
  Accepted Findings for Review Record
  - Catalog/Batch objects are missing from v4.1.
  - Catalog Coverage Ledger is missing.
  - Cross-batch Entity persistence is missing.
  - Scope / Coverage / Completion / Verification are not explicitly separated at catalog level.
  - SKU-level lifecycle state across multiple batches is missing.
  - Cumulative cross-batch budget accounting is missing.
  - Global Supplier Discovery vs Region-Aware SKU Matching can be made explicit.
  Audit Correction — Validation Language
  Claude's Part 5 says “Tests Passed” and “pass by construction”, while Part 8 and Part 7 correctly state that the new multi-batch mechanisms have not yet been exercised in a live v4.2 runtime.
  Therefore the authoritative review interpretation is:
  - Structural/design check: addressed / provisionally passed.
  - Live runtime validation: not yet performed.
  - Integrated multi-batch validation: pending.
  The stronger wording “Tests Passed” must not be used as evidence of runtime correctness for v4.2.
  Artifact Verification Status
  Claude reports that a complete v4.2 Candidate file was produced, but the full artifact itself has not been independently verified in this ChatGPT workspace from the current exchange. Until the exact v4.2 text/file is available and checked against the claimed insertions, v4.2 should be recorded as Claude-reported Candidate, not as a verified Prompt artifact.
  Governance
  No Canonical promotion and no governance change occurred in this cycle. The standing bootstrap approval decision remains separate and unresolved.

  The project uses a controlled  Prompt Working Reference  for active Prompt text and draft evolution.
  - Claude  is the direct editor of Prompt Working Reference content.
  - ChatGPT  reads, audits, tests, and records findings; it does not directly rewrite that Prompt Working Reference.
  - Prompt Working Reference changes are  working draft changes , not approval or Canonical promotion.
  - User approval remains mandatory  for promotion to Approved/Canonical.
  - Neither agent may modify Canonical authority, approval status, promotion gates, or governance rules through the Prompt Working Reference.
  - When a proposed Prompt change affects governance, authority, ownership, permissions, lifecycle, or promotion rules, it is  Governance-Touching  and requires a user decision.
  The Prompt Working Reference must never be confused with the protected Canonical Registry state.
  Core invariants
  Review ≠ Authority
  Draft ≠ Approved
  Proposal ≠ Decision
  Workspace ≠ Canonical Registry
  There may be  zero or one Approved Prompt version  at any time; there must never be more than one. Until the first approval, the Approved state remains empty.
  Version lifecycle
  Draft → Candidate → Review → Revision → User Decision → Approved → Superseded
  Rejected versions remain recorded and are never silently deleted.
  Promotion gate
  A Candidate may be promoted only when:
  - Its Parent Version ID matches the current Approved Version ID.
  - Required review and tests are recorded.
  - Any Governance-Touching change has received the required user decision.
  - The user explicitly approves promotion.
  If the Parent Version ID is stale, promotion is blocked and the Candidate must be rebased.
  Conflict rule
  Two Candidates from the same Parent remain separate sibling versions.
  They are not auto-merged.
  A synthesis version may be created only as a new version that explicitly records which changes came from which parents/candidates.
  Change classification
  - Existing Project Rule
  - Evidence-Based Repair
  - Proposed Governance Decision
  Synchronization rule
  The transport between ChatGPT and Claude may be manual or automated in the future. The authority model does not depend on transport.
  The Canonical Registry is the only authoritative reference for the approved Prompt and its version lineage.
  Required control records
  This page contains the controlled version and decision records:
  - Version Registry
  - Decision Log
  All future Prompt development must reference the current Approved Version before drafting a new Candidate.

  Decision Log — Supplier Intelligence

  🧪 Pre-Claude Repair Audit — v4.0 → v4.1 Candidate
  Status
  Pre-Claude repair completed against the current v4.0 Candidate. v4.0 remains unchanged as historical baseline.
  Evidence Base
  - Current v4.0 prompt was read in full from the uploaded file.
  - Canonical Registry currently has no Approved Prompt.
  - Existing Version Registry already records v4.0 as Candidate and lists remaining gaps: frontier/stopping semantics, Entity Resolution discovery blind spot, runtime request mutation, price-decision policy, branch control, and freshness semantics.
  - External research was used to validate the repair directions, not to replace project-specific evidence.
  Repairs Applied in v4.1 Candidate
  1. Runtime scope lock
  User objective, constraints, price ceiling, target region, required attributes, and comparison basis are immutable for the run. Discoveries can create branches/hypotheses but cannot silently redefine the request.
  2. Run Contract and action-level auditability
  Each run now requires Run ID, prompt version, scope, evidence standard, complexity class, budget, coverage obligations, and known tool limits. Each Research Action must record its exact query/target, method, timestamp, branch, yield, retrieval status, affected objects, selection rationale, next-state effect, and budget debit.
  3. Evidence integrity
  Search snippets/previews/cached summaries are explicitly downgraded to lead/context unless the underlying source is unavailable. Material claims should rely on directly retrieved source content or structured first-party evidence whenever possible.
  4. Freshness semantics
  Freshness is separated from price-evidence strength. A directly observed price may be Freshness=Unknown without creating a semantic contradiction. Live transactional states can support Current when there is no material staleness signal; recent observation alone is not sufficient when archival/stale signals exist.
  5. Guarantee semantics
  “No Documented Guarantee” is now allowed only after the relevant accessible terms/offer documentation were actually reviewed. Retrieval failure or incomplete access yields Unknown.
  6. Transaction verification
  Performed-and-Evidenced now requires an actual authorized transaction/test with date, offer, amount, fees, outcome, and evidence. Reading a page never counts as transaction verification.
  7. Entity Resolution candidate-generation blind spot
  A Stage 0 candidate-generation layer was added. Blocking is explicitly a search-space reduction mechanism, not a non-match decision. High-value candidates without shared blocking signals remain eligible for deeper resolution.
  8. Coverage obligations
  The controller remains adaptive, but objective-driven coverage groups must be seeded so one highly productive discovery family cannot mask an unsearched materially relevant route. This is coverage protection, not equal-depth quota.
  9. Search strategy diversity
  Near-duplicate queries no longer count as meaningful exploration. Reformulation must change a material dimension such as terminology, role, channel, language, market, SKU, or seed.
  10. Search budget control
  Run complexity classes C1–C4 now have disclosed default action caps and a shared reserve. A hypothesis receives a minimum exploration floor when budget permits. A single branch is capped at 35% unless it is Critical for a live contradiction/headline claim.
  11. Stopping states
  Branch termination is now explicitly distinguished as Saturated, Retrieval-Limited, Budget-Limited, or Scope-Excluded. Retrieval and budget limits are never mislabeled as saturation.
  12. Exclusion handling
  Excluded entities are quarantined from commercial ranking and upstream inference but preserved in the research record, preventing loss of audit history and reducing false-positive damage.
  13. Effective acquisition cost
  Known mandatory taxes/charges are added explicitly; unknown mandatory components remain Unknown and never become zero.
  14. Output audit record
  Every run now ends with established facts, unresolved items, inaccessible routes, exclusions, counter-evidence survival, stopping rationale, unsearched material routes, potential conclusion-changing evidence, scope expansions, and tool limitations.
  Architectural Alternatives Reviewed
  - Monolithic v4.x with explicit control contracts and stronger state semantics.
  - Split Controller + Evidence Ledger architecture, with orchestration externalized from the research prompt.
  - Parallel specialist agents (discovery, verification, entity resolution, pricing) sharing a structured evidence graph.
  For the current  z.ai/Notion  setup, the first approach is the least disruptive; the second is the stronger longer-term architecture if external orchestration and persistent state become available. The third adds complexity and coordination cost and should be justified by measured workload, not assumed.
  External Research Signals
  DeepSearchQA explicitly evaluates multi-step research on systematic collation, deduplication/entity resolution, and stopping criteria, and reports persistent weaknesses in balancing recall and precision. BrowseComp similarly emphasizes persistence, strategic browsing, and synthesis across many sites. Current provenance work on LLM agents emphasizes claim-level evidence tracing and execution provenance rather than final-answer accuracy alone. Entity-resolution literature warns that early blocking can lose true matches, making blocking recall a real error surface. These findings support the v4.1 repair directions above.
  Remaining Governance Gap
  The current Canonical Registry promotion gate requires Candidate.Parent Version ID to equal the current Approved Version ID, but the registry currently has no Approved Prompt. This makes first approval impossible without a bootstrap exception.
  Proposed rule — NOT AUTO-APPROVED:
  When no Approved Prompt exists, the first approval may promote a Candidate that passes the required review/tests and is explicitly approved by the user, provided its parent is the latest validated lineage candidate. After the first approval, the normal Parent=Current Approved rule applies.
  This must remain an Open Governance Decision until the user approves it.
  Pre-Claude Position
  v4.0 should not be sent to Claude as the sole baseline anymore. The current object for validation should be v4.1 Candidate, while v4.0 remains the historical comparator. Claude's role is now to independently test the repaired candidate and try to break it.
  Next Handoff
  Send Claude the dedicated validation brief for v4.1. Do not request Notion edits. Return the full test, findings, counter-evidence, regression results, and any new repair candidate.
  🔄 Interaction Archive — Supplier Intelligence
  🔁 Operating Protocol — Notion-First ChatGPT ↔ Claude Relay
  Purpose
  Notion is the persistent system of record. Chat is only the transport channel.
  The user should only need to send the current work/request to ChatGPT. ChatGPT prepares the next handoff. The user pastes the prepared handoff to Claude. Claude returns its result. The user pastes Claude's result back to ChatGPT. ChatGPT archives the result, audits it, updates the working state, and prepares the next handoff.
  Project-Scope Authorization
  All Notion pages, databases, records, and artifacts that are directly linked to the  Adaptive Supplier Intelligence Research Engine  are considered authorized working surfaces for the project's normal operating cycle. ChatGPT may read, relate, archive, update, organize, and maintain these project-linked surfaces in whatever way is operationally appropriate for ChatGPT↔Claude collaboration, while preserving authority boundaries and user approval gates. This authorization applies only to project-linked material; unrelated projects, files, pages, databases, and records remain outside scope unless the user explicitly authorizes their use.
  Authoritative Architecture
  ChatGPT Workspace  = review, audit, analysis, validation, repair control.
  Claude Workspace  = research, development, experimentation, draft/candidate production.
  Canonical Registry  = only authoritative location for an Approved Prompt.
  Interaction Archive  = immutable working history of every cycle, handoff, response, audit, decision, and canonical update.
  Edit Authority Matrix
  Boundary Rules
  - Each agent edits only the workspace it owns.
  - The  Prompt Working Reference  is the only shared prompt-development surface that Claude may directly edit. Claude may change prompt text and draft evolution there, but may not alter Canonical authority, approval status, governance rules, or unrelated records.
  - ChatGPT does not directly rewrite Claude's workspace or Claude's Prompt Working Reference. It audits, identifies repairs, and records findings in its own workspace.
  - ChatGPT may update project-linked operational records, archives, audits, and review state, but it must not promote a Candidate, mark a Prompt Approved, or silently change governance.
  - The Canonical Registry remains protected: its authority, approval state, version lineage, and promotion gates are never changed by either agent without the user's explicit decision.
  - Neither agent may delete historical records, silently merge competing Candidates, or overwrite authoritative source text with an inferred reconstruction.
  - When a change affects governance, ownership, authority, promotion, permissions, or the collaboration protocol, classify it as  Governance-Touching  and route it to the user for decision.
  - If ownership or edit authority is ambiguous, do not edit; classify the item as  Needs User Decision  and preserve the ambiguity in the record.
  Core Loop
  User Input
  → ChatGPT analyzes
  → ChatGPT archives the step
  → ChatGPT prepares one copy-ready Claude Handoff
  → User sends Handoff to Claude
  → Claude produces research/draft/test output
  → User pastes Claude output to ChatGPT
  → ChatGPT archives Claude output
  → ChatGPT audits and reconciles it against the current state
  → ChatGPT records findings/changes/open decisions
  → ChatGPT prepares the next Claude Handoff
  → repeat
  Token Preservation Rule
  Do not send the entire historical conversation to Claude on every cycle.
  Each handoff must contain only the minimum sufficient context:
  - Current objective
  - Current version/state
  - Relevant prior findings
  - Exact task for this cycle
  - Tests/constraints that matter
  - Required output format
  - References to the persistent Notion records
  The detailed history remains in Notion.
  Archive Rule
  Every meaningful transition is archived as a separate Interaction Archive record:
  - User Input
  - ChatGPT Analysis
  - Claude Handoff
  - Claude Response
  - ChatGPT Audit
  - Decision
  - Canonical Update
  No step is considered part of the durable project state until it is archived.
  Handoff Rule
  ChatGPT must produce exactly one primary copy-ready message for Claude per cycle.
  The handoff must identify:
  - Version ID
  - Parent Version ID
  - Current state
  - Objective
  - Scope
  - Task
  - Evidence requirements
  - Tests
  - Constraints
  - Required output
  - Notion archive references
  Claude must not be asked to edit the Canonical Registry during validation unless explicitly authorized.
  Response Rule
  When Claude responds, the user should paste the response into ChatGPT without manually summarizing it.
  ChatGPT then:
  - Archives the raw response.
  - Extracts findings.
  - Validates claims/evidence.
  - Compares against the current Candidate/Canonical state.
  - Identifies repairs.
  - Separates technical repairs from governance decisions.
  - Updates Notion records.
  - Prepares the next single handoff message.
  Governance
  No Candidate becomes Approved automatically.
  No historical record is deleted merely because it is superseded.
  No governance rule is inferred from a technical suggestion.
  No competing Candidate is silently merged.
  No stale Candidate is promoted without rebase.
  Current State
  Current Candidate: v4.1
  v4.0: preserved as historical baseline comparator.
  Approved Prompt: none.
  Open Governance Decision: bootstrap rule for first approval when Approved state is empty.
  Current next step: independent Claude validation of v4.1.

  Primary Reference Rule
  The following two materials are now the  primary source pair  for understanding and developing the Supplier Intelligence system:
  - Primary Reference A — Original  z.ai  Supplier Research Prompt
  - File:  z_ai_supplier_research_prompt.md
  - Role: baseline for the original research-execution logic, rules, data model, verification model, discovery methodology, comparison rules, stopping rules, and output structure.
  - Primary Reference B — The original research text/material submitted together with the Prompt
  - Role: baseline for the actual research objective, requested subject matter, product/service context, intended outputs, and original execution context.
  - Do not replace, rewrite, or reinterpret this source merely because a later Prompt version is cleaner.
  Pairing Rule
  Prompt + Original Research Text = Primary Reference Pair.
  Later versions may improve the system, but they must not silently erase the intent, requirements, or important distinctions contained in either member of the pair.
  Development Stage Register
  Version Lineage
  Original Primary Reference Pair
  →  v2
  →  v3
  →  v4.0
  →  v4.1 Candidate
  →  Claude Validation / Further Candidate
  →  User Decision
  →  Approved / Canonical
  No stage may be silently skipped when the skip would remove material history.
  Stage Governance
  Primary references
  The original Prompt and the original research text remain preserved as the historical and conceptual baseline.
  Working development
  Claude may develop Prompt text in the  Prompt Working Reference  and Claude Workspace.
  ChatGPT reviews, tests, audits, and records findings in the ChatGPT-owned surfaces.
  Canonical authority
  The Canonical Registry is the protected authority for the  Approved Prompt .
  No Candidate becomes Approved automatically.
  History preservation
  Historical versions and records are preserved.
  A later version supersedes a previous version only through the controlled lifecycle.
  Change Classification
  Every meaningful development change must be classified as one of:
  - Existing Project Rule
  - Evidence-Based Repair
  - Proposed Governance Decision
  - Prompt Evolution
  - Research Finding
  - Unresolved
  Never convert a proposal into a rule without the required authority.
  Current State
  - Primary Reference Pair:  locked as baseline references .
  - v4.0: preserved historical comparator.
  - v4.1: current Candidate.
  - Approved Prompt:  none currently recorded .
  - Claude: owns direct Prompt Working Reference editing.
  - ChatGPT: owns review, audit, validation, and operational archive.
  - User: final authority for approval, promotion, rollback, and governance decisions.
  Required Rule for Future Development
  Before changing the Prompt, compare the proposed change against:
  A. Original Prompt
  B. Original Research Text
  C. Current Prompt Candidate
  D. Recorded project decisions
  E. Recorded test failures and repairs
  A change is valid only when its purpose and impact are explicit.
  Do not optimize away an original requirement merely because it is inconvenient or difficult to implement.
  Do not treat a later Prompt version as proof that an earlier requirement was invalid.
  Core Principle
  The original Prompt defines the original operating design.
  The original research text defines the original research intent and execution context.
  Version history defines how the system evolved.
  Canonical Registry defines what is officially approved.
  These four functions must remain distinct.
  ✅ Approved Operating Distinction — Prompt Layers
  Decision ID:   Governance-Compiler-Separation-1.0

  - Primary Supplier Research Prompt  — the operational research engine for Supplier Intelligence.
  - Claude Handoff Prompt  — the coordination/review/development layer used to direct Claude work on the project and its Prompt.
  - EXECUTION-PROMPT COMPILER — student services  — a separate generic Prompt Compiler reference. It is  not  part of the Supplier Intelligence engine and must not be inserted into it automatically.
  Historical Handoff Rule
  The older Claude handoff remains a  historical reference . Reuse is permitted only after reconciliation with the current project state, the Primary Reference Pair, v4.1 Candidate, and recorded governance decisions.
  Non-Mixing Rule
  Do not silently merge these layers, transfer unrelated modules, or import unrelated project material into Supplier Intelligence. Any proposed structural or governance change remains subject to the existing authority and approval rules.
  This section records the user's explicit approval and does not promote any Prompt Candidate to Canonical status.

SKU Separation Rule
Product/Service Family ≠ Actual SKU.
الفئة تحدد مجال البحث، بينما الـSKU الفعلي يجب أن يحدد المنتج/الخدمة القابلة للمقارنة مع سماتها التجارية المؤثرة في القيمة، مثل الخطة/الإصدار، المدة أو القيمة الاسمية، الكمية، المنطقة، التفعيل، نوع الحساب، التسليم، والقيود، بحسب الفئة.
لا يجوز اختيار  eSIM  أو  SaaS/AI  أو  Gift Card  أو  API Pack  من واجهة التشغيل باعتبارها SKU فعليًا إلا إذا كان طلب التشغيل نفسه يحدد المنتج/الخدمة والخصائص المطلوبة.
Development Control
المعلومات الجديدة القادمة من تشغيل  z.ai  تُعامل أولًا كـ Runtime Observation / Evidence . لا تتحول تلقائيًا إلى قاعدة Prompt أو Governance Rule قبل المراجعة.
